TL;DR: 该论文提出Instruct-to-Act系统，将VLM规划器（生成高层文本指令）与世界模型控制器（高频低延迟控制）结合，通过指令重标注和联合优化训练控制器使其可被语言指令指导，在七个具身环境中验证了性能提升和模块可替换性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练指令微调的VLM能够从指令和观察中生成高层计划，但在陌生环境中难以将其实现为可靠的低延迟动作序列；而世界模型控制器擅长快速观察到动作的控制，但缺乏开放式的任务引导能力。两者优势互补，需要一种方法将它们结合起来。

Method: 构建Instruct-to-Act系统：(1) VLM规划器生成稀疏、高延迟的高层文本指令；(2) 训练世界模型控制器在条件化这些指令时以高频自主执行动作；(3) 通过将控制器策略rollout片段用合成指令重标注，并联合优化行为克隆目标与原有的奖励最大化和世界建模目标来训练语言可指令的控制器。

Result: 在七个具身环境中评估，包括三个多智能体环境（VLM规划器通过语言协调，控制器作为执行器）。在匹配的观察与动作空间下，该解耦方法一致优于仅控制器和直接VLM动作生成变体，保持快速控制，且可无需微调即可替换不同的预训练VLM规划器，在七个任务中的六个上仍与强视觉-语言-动作模型和多智能体RL基线相当。

Conclusion: 将VLM规划与预训练世界模型控制器解耦结合是一种有效的架构，既能保持高频低延迟控制，又能获得开放式的任务引导能力，同时支持规划器的灵活替换，为具身智能体任务规划与控制提供了可行的设计范式。

Abstract: Recent work shows that pre-trained, instruction-tuned vision-language models (VLMs) perform well at mapping from instructions and observations to high-level plans, but struggle to realize such plans as reliable low-latency action sequences in unfamiliar environments. At the same time, world-model controllers excel at fast observation-to-action control, but lack open-ended task guidance. In this work, we combine these strengths into a single system, Instruct-to-Act, where we train a world-model controller to act autonomously at high frequency when conditioned on sparse, higher-latency, and high-level text instructions generated by a VLM planner. To train controllers to be language-instructable, we relabel segments of controller policy rollouts with synthetic instructions and jointly optimize a behavior-cloning objective along with existing reward-maximizing and world-modeling objectives. We evaluate our proposed approach across seven embodied environments, including three multi-agent environments where VLM planners coordinate through language while trained controllers serve as their actuators. Under matched observation and action spaces, our decoupled approach consistently outperforms controller-only and direct VLM action-generation variants, preserves fast control, and lets us swap in different pretrained VLM planners without fine-tuning, while remaining competitive with strong vision-language-action and multi-agent RL baselines on six of seven tasks.

</details>


### [132] [Assessing mentalization in humans and large language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26291)
*Aamir Sohail,Xintong Zhong,Arkady Konovalov,Patricia L. Lockwood,Lei Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究通过两个经济博弈任务和认知计算建模，测试了四类大语言模型（DeepSeek、GPT-4.1、GPT-5和Gemini 2.0 Flash，共2,099个智能体）的心智化能力，并以251名人类参与者作为对照。结果发现LLMs表现出与人类一致的心智化的行为与计算特征，但不同模型提供商和规模存在显著差异；策略性提示能普遍提升性能；GPT-5能灵活调节递归推理深度以适应对手复杂度，表现优于人类。研究将认知计算建模确立为评估人机比较智能的正式方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然已有研究显示LLMs在心理理论（theory-of-mind）任务上表现出与人类一致的行为，但尚未明确这些模型是否能够通过心智化来指导适应性行为。本研究旨在填补这一空白，探究LLMs是否具备利用心智化引导自适应决策的深层认知能力。

Method: 采用两种经济博弈任务（economic games）结合认知计算建模（cognitive computational modeling），用以揭示LLMs在心智化过程中的潜在策略。测试了来自四个模型家族（DeepSeek、GPT-4.1、GPT-5和Gemini 2.0 Flash）的2,099个智能体，面对不同复杂度的对手，并检验一种旨在引发策略推理的提示策略能否提升表现，以251名人类参与者作为基准对照。

Result: 1) 在两种博弈任务中，LLMs均表现出清晰的心智化行为与计算特征，但不同模型供应商和规模之间存在显著差异；2) 策略性提示总体上通过诱导更复杂的推理提升了性能，但收益程度因任务而异；3) GPT-5能够根据对手复杂度灵活调整递归推理深度，在表现上超越人类参与者。

Conclusion: 不同LLMs在心智化能力上存在差异，认知计算建模可作为评估人类与机器比较智能的正式方法，为理解和衡量机器智能的认知基础提供了新的分析框架。

Abstract: Mentalization - the ability to infer others' beliefs and intentions to guide one's own choices - is a key cognitive function underlying human social interactions. Large language models (LLMs) demonstrate behaviour consistent with humans on theory-of-mind tasks, yet whether these models can guide adaptive behaviour through mentalization is unknown. Here we use two economic games with cognitive computational modeling to uncover the latent strategies underlying mentalization in LLMs. We tested individual LLM agents across four model families, DeepSeek, GPT-4.1, GPT-5 and Gemini 2.0 Flash (N = 2,099), against opponents of varying sophistication and examined whether a prompting strategy designed to elicit strategic reasoning improved performance. We benchmarked results against human participants (N = 251) as a comparative measure. Across both games, LLMs showed clear behavioural and computational signatures of mentalizing that differed markedly by model provider and size. Strategic prompting generally improved performance by inducing more sophisticated reasoning, yet the extent of the benefit differed across the two tasks. Last, GPT-5 agents flexibly adapted their recursive depth of reasoning to increasingly sophisticated opponents, demonstrating superior performance to human participants. Collectively, we demonstrate different capacities for mentalization across LLMs, and highlight cognitive computational modeling as a formal method for assessing comparative intelligence across humans and machines.

</details>


### [133] [Approved Too Late: Verdict Staleness in LLM-Guarded Self-Adaptive Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26306)
*Ilai Shraga,Roei Eshel,Lior Gorelik*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究自适应系统中大语言模型防护栏的"判决新鲜度"问题，即防护栏在检查时正确但执行时过期的TOCTOU风险。作者区分了三种衡量判决新鲜度的指标，提出Freshness-Bounded Shield（FBS）方法，在五个可复现环境中将批准过期率从3.4-24.7%降至0-1.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM防护栏在自适性系统中进行安全批准时，判决在检查时刻是正确的，但到实际执行时刻可能已经失效（TOCTOU危害）。这一时间差导致的"判决过期"问题尚未被系统研究，缺乏定义、量化方法和缓解手段。

Method: 1) 提出三种互补的新鲜度指标：固定动作回放下的全候选判决变化率、闭环轨迹上oracle标注的批准过期率、以及judge条件化的使用时无效性。2) 提出FBS框架，利用每个许可的安全侧裕度和近期特征波动性估计其有效期限，无需显式植物动力学模型。

Result: 在五个可复现SAS环境中，全候选判决变化率在8步重放下达5.3-48.4%；FBS在相同设置下将oracle标注的批准过期率从3.4-24.7%降至0-1.8%；四个LLM judge的审计显示每个批准流都存在非零的judge条件化使用时无效性。

Conclusion: 提出"新鲜度契约"：每个批准必须在检查时正确且在使用时仍然有效。该契约可作为LLM防护栏设计的规范约束，要求系统同时保证检查时正确性与使用时有效性。

Abstract: A large language model (LLM) guardrail for a self-adaptive system (SAS) may issue an approval that is correct at check time but stale by actuation. This creates an Execute-stage time-of-check to time-of-use (TOCTOU) hazard. We study verdict freshness: whether a guardrail verdict remains valid when used. We distinguish three quantities that answer different questions: all-candidate verdict change under fixed-action replay, oracle-labeled approval expiry on recorded closed-loop trajectories, and judge-conditioned use-time invalidity. Across five reproducible SAS environments, all-candidate verdict-change rates span 5.3-48.4% at a common replay shift of eight simulator steps. We introduce the Freshness-Bounded Shield (FBS), which estimates each approval's validity horizon from its safe-side margin and recent feature volatility, without an explicit plant-dynamics model. Using fixed settings documented in the artifact, FBS reduces oracle-labeled approval-expiry rates from 3.4-24.7% to 0-1.8% at the same shift. A separate audit of four LLM judges finds nonzero judge-conditioned use-time invalidity in every approval stream. We formulate a freshness contract: every approval must be correct at check time and remain valid at use time.

</details>


### [134] [FaithSieve: Fine-Grained Evaluation of Math Proofs with Faithful Formal Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26310)
*Ziyu Wang,Qiming Dai,Yishan Wu,Zaiwen Wen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出FaithSieve，一个基于Lean的形式化辅助框架，用于自然语言数学证明的细粒度评估和首个错误定位。该框架将粗粒度证明步骤分解为局部推理单元，提取类型化证明义务，并通过形式化评估智能体验证，同时用语义对齐评分把关，仅在形式化表述忠实保持原始声明的上下文、对象和逻辑形式时才纳入Lean证据。在350道奥林匹克数学题上达到81.43%的准确率，在200道大学数学题上达到84.5%的准确率，均优于直接判断基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型已能生成复杂多步数学证明，但可靠地判断正确性并定位早期逻辑错误仍是关键挑战。现有评估方法主要依赖基于模型的自然语言判断，常忽略局部推理缺陷；形式化定理证明器如Lean虽能提供严谨验证，但用于评估非形式化文本时存在局部性（prover可能通过证明过宽目标绕过局部缺陷）和语义错配（自动形式化语句可能偏离原始数学意图）两大问题。

Method: FaithSieve框架包含三个核心环节：(1) 将粗粒度证明步骤分解为局部推理单元；(2) 提取类型化证明义务（typed proof obligations）；(3) 通过形式化评估智能体验证这些义务。关键创新是引入语义对齐评分作为形式化验证的门控机制，只有当形式化表述在上下文、对象和逻辑形式上忠实保留原始声称时才采纳Lean证据，从而解决形式化与非形式化之间的语义漂移问题。

Result: 构建了两个专家验证的数据集ProofLoc-Olympiad（350题，奥林匹克数学）和ProofLoc-University（200题，覆盖六个高级数学领域）。在Olympiad数据集上，FaithSieve使用GPT-5.4骨干取得81.43%的精确首个错误定位准确率，优于直接判断基线72.29%；在University基准上达到84.5%的精确准确率，优于直接判断的75.0%。

Conclusion: 将证明分解为细粒度单元并用忠实的形式化证据进行锚定，能显著提升自然语言数学推理评估的可靠性。该工作为利用形式化定理证明器评估非形式化推理提供了有效的范式，克服了形式化验证在局部性和语义匹配方面的关键障碍。

Abstract: Large language models can now generate complex, multi-step mathematical proofs, but reliably determining their correctness and localizing early logical errors remains a critical challenge. Existing evaluation approaches largely depend on model-based natural-language judgments, which often overlook local reasoning gaps. While formal theorem provers like Lean offer a path to rigorous verification, using them to evaluate informal text requires solving locality and semantic mismatches: a prover might bypass a local flaw by proving an overly broad target, or validate an auto-formalized statement that drifts from the original mathematical intent. To address this, we introduce FaithSieve, a Lean-assisted framework for fine-grained evaluation of natural-language mathematical proofs. FaithSieve decomposes coarse proof steps into local reasoning units, extracts typed proof obligations, and verifies them through a formal evaluation agent. Formal validation is gated by semantic alignment scoring, so Lean evidence is incorporated only when the formal statement faithfully preserves the context, objects, and logical form of the original claim. We construct two expert-verified datasets, ProofLoc-Olympiad and ProofLoc-University, to benchmark first-error localization. On the 350-problem Olympiad dataset, FaithSieve using a GPT-5.4 backbone achieves 81.43% exact first-error accuracy, outperforming the direct-judging baseline of 72.29%. Furthermore, on the 200-problem ProofLoc-University benchmark spanning six advanced domains, FaithSieve reaches 84.5% exact accuracy, compared to 75.0% for the direct judge. Our work demonstrates that decomposing proofs into fine-grained units and grounding them with faithful formal evidence significantly improves reliable evaluation of natural-language reasoning.

</details>


### [135] [ProofEvolve: Neuro-Symbolic Evolution for Formal Automated Theorem Proving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26334)
*Wenqian Ye,Ziwei Guan,Eric Xie,Bohan Liu,Shivani Modi,Buyun Zhang,Ellie Dingqiao Wen,Henry Kautz,Aidong Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 ProofEvolve，一个将神经模型与符号验证相结合的定理证明框架，通过显式演化经过形式化验证的符号证明结构（如分解、修复、模式重组）来拓展知识边界。其核心创新在于维护两个层次的已验证闭包：问题内通过行为索引档案演化部分 AND-OR 证明 DAG，跨问题通过内核校验的模式提取构建持久模式库。在三个竞赛级 Lean 基准上取得了最高平均解题率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化定理证明为科学发现中的递归自我改进提供了自然基础，但现有神经证明器未能完全保留这种递归结构。主要痛点包括：(1) 现有方法要么通过昂贵的权重更新将证明经验嵌入模型参数，要么仅将验证过的中间推导保留在当前问题内部，缺乏跨问题的知识累积；(2) 现有方法严重依赖稀疏的整证明反馈，忽视了不成功部分尝试中蕴含的有效发现。

Method: ProofEvolve 是一个神经-符号混合框架。神经模型负责提出变异算子，包括分解（decompositions）、修复（repairs）和模式重组（schema recombinations）；符号 Lean 内核验证每一步证明转换。在演化循环中，框架计算证明有向无环图（DAG）上的已验证闭包。问题内部：演化部分 AND-OR 证明 DAG，并存储在行为索引档案（behaviorally indexed archive）中；跨问题：通过内核校验的模式提取，将新证明的子 DAG 加入持久模式库，并通过类型化模式重组继承已解结果，每个残余前提暴露为新子目标。这一过程保留了不完整尝试中的已验证结果，并在不削弱形式化可靠性的前提下使其可用于后续证明。

Result: 在三个竞赛级 Lean 基准（competition-level Lean benchmarks）上，ProofEvolve 在所有被评估的证明系统中取得了最高的平均解题率（solve rate）。

Conclusion: ProofEvolve 通过显式演化形式化验证的符号证明结构，有效弥合了现有神经证明器在递归自我改进方面的差距。它解决了三个关键问题：跨问题的知识复用（持久模式库）、不完整尝试中有效信息的保留（行为索引档案与已验证闭包）、以及局部反馈的利用。最重要的是，该框架在增强知识累积能力的同时维持了严格的形式化可靠性保障。

Abstract: Automated theorem proving offers a natural foundation for recursive self-improvement in scientific discovery. However, existing neural provers do not fully preserve this recursive structure, where the learning process should be self-improving over time. Existing methods either embed proof experience into model parameters through expensive weight updates, or keep verified intermediate deductions only within the current problem. In addition, these methods also heavily rely on sparse whole-proof feedback, even when unsuccessful partial attempts contain useful discoveries. To close the gap, we propose ProofEvolve, a neuro-symbolic framework that evolves explicit, formally verified symbolic proof structures with neural models to decisively expand the knowledge boundary. In this framework, the neural model proposes variation operators, including decompositions, repairs, and schema recombinations. The symbolic Lean kernel verifies every proof transition. Over the evolution loops, ProofEvolve computes verified closure over the resulting proof directed acyclic graphs (DAGs). Within each problem, ProofEvolve evolves partial AND-OR proof DAGs in a behaviorally indexed archive. Across problems, kernel-checked schema extraction adds newly proved sub-DAGs to a persistent schema library. Proof DAGs inherit the solved results through typed schema recombination, with every residual premise exposed as a new subgoal. This evolutionary process preserves verified results from incomplete attempts and makes them available for later proofs without weakening formal soundness. Across three competition-level Lean benchmarks, ProofEvolve achieves the highest average solve rate among the evaluated proof systems.

</details>


### [136] [Fine-Tuning of Transformer models with Frames](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26430)
*Harshavardhan Adepu,Li Zhang,Sanjiv Kumar,Vikas Singh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FrameFT是一种基于融合框架(Fusion Frame)基础的参数高效微调方法，通过稀疏系数矩阵建模参数更新，显著降低内存和计算开销，在语言和视觉任务上达到或超越现有PEFT方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PEFT方法（如LoRA）虽然有效，但内存需求随模型尺寸线性增长（O(dr)），在大型预训练模型微调时仍存在显著的资源瓶颈，需要更高效的参数更新表示方式来降低内存占用。

Method: 提出FrameFT方法，利用算法生成的Fusion Frame基础来表示参数更新ΔW，使用稀疏系数矩阵建模。Fusion Frames可在各层之间共享，仅存储和优化基展开的稀疏系数，从而大幅降低内存占用；同时利用系数矩阵的稀疏结构和Fusion Frames的内在稀疏性获得计算收益，并提供形式化收敛性分析。

Result: 在语言任务和监督微调基准测试上，FrameFT达到或超过现有的SOTA PEFT技术，同时所需的可训练参数数量显著更少；此外还验证了该方法在视觉模型上的应用效果。

Conclusion: FrameFT通过稀疏系数和共享的Fusion Frame基础，在不牺牲性能的前提下大幅减少了可训练参数数量和内存需求，为大规模预训练模型的高效微调提供了一种新的有效方案。

Abstract: Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning (PEFT) strategies such as Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) are effective solutions for fine-tuning large-scale pre-trained models; however, their memory requirements scale with the size of the model, $\mathcal{O}(dr)$, where $d$ is the model's hidden dimension and $r$ is the rank. Our proposal, FrameFT, models the parameter update $ΔW$ with a sparse coefficient matrix in a Fusion Frame basis. Fusion Frames can be generated algorithmically and shared across model layers, enabling very efficient updates. Only the sparse coefficients of the basis expansion are stored/optimized, reducing the memory footprint. The sparse structure of the coefficient matrix in FrameFT and the sparsity in the Fusion Frames give large compute benefits, and our analysis provides formal convergence results. We evaluate the idea across a suite of supervised fine-tuning benchmarks, focusing on language tasks, but also report application to vision models. Our experiments show that FrameFT achieves performance on par with/exceeding state-of-the-art PEFT techniques, but needs far fewer trainable parameters.

</details>


### [137] [Don't Overthink, Don't Underthink: Toward Adaptive Reasoning in Agentic AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26442)
*Md Jueal Mia,M. Hadi Amini*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出智能体AI系统中的推理分配问题是下一代LLM的核心挑战。现有推理控制方法（如固定token预算、预执行难度估计）不适用于动态变化的智能体工作流。作者在MATH-500和GAIA基准上评估了过度推理（over-reasoning）和推理不足（under-reasoning）两种失败模式，发现过度推理导致高计算成本却无相应精度提升，推理不足则与错误或不完整解决方案一致。研究为自适应推理机制的未来工作提供了动机。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM推理增强方法（如延长思维链）大多依赖固定或预分配的推理控制策略，且仅在独立推理基准上评估，未考虑智能体AI系统中推理需求随规划、工具使用、记忆检索和智能体间交互动态变化的特性。这种假设不匹配导致推理分配失衡：推理过多造成计算浪费和延迟，推理不足则产生不完整或不正确的解决方案。

Method: 作者将推理分配问题形式化为过度推理和推理不足两种失败模式，并在MATH-500和GAIA公共验证基准上进行评估。评估指标包括工具决策延迟、token消耗、token限制耗尽情况和答案正确性，通过这些指标对两种失败模式进行量化分析。

Result: 实验结果显示：过度推理案例与更高的计算成本相关，但未带来相应的准确性提升；推理不足案例则始终与错误或不完整的解决方案相关。这表明推理总量的多少不是关键，关键在于推理应如何根据任务需求动态调整分配。

Conclusion: 下一代智能体AI的核心挑战不是LLM应该推理多少，而是如何根据动态变化的任务需求自适应地分配推理资源。研究为设计自适应推理机制（adaptive reasoning mechanisms）提供了实证基础和明确的研究方向。

Abstract: Recent advances in Large Language Models (LLMs) have shown that increased inference-time reasoning can improve performance on complex tasks. However, many existing approaches rely on fixed or preallocated reasoning controls, such as fixed token budgets, pre-execution difficulty estimates, or activation-space interventions, and are often evaluated on standalone reasoning benchmarks rather than full agentic workflows. These assumptions may not hold in agentic AI systems, where reasoning requirements evolve dynamically through planning, tool use, memory retrieval, and agent-to-agent interactions. Consequently, reasoning can become either excessive or insufficient, resulting in unnecessary computation, increased latency, planning drift, excessive tool use, or incomplete solutions. We argue that a major challenge for next-generation agentic AI is not merely how much reasoning a language model should perform, but how it should allocate reasoning according to evolving task demands. We characterize over-reasoning and under-reasoning as recurring failure modes of misallocated reasoning and evaluate them on MATH-500 and the GAIA public validation benchmark. Using tool-decision latency, token consumption, token-limit exhaustion, and answer correctness, our results suggest that cases classified as over-reasoning are associated with higher computational cost without proportional accuracy gains, whereas cases classified as under-reasoning are consistently associated with incorrect or incomplete solutions. These findings motivate future research on adaptive reasoning mechanisms for agentic AI.

</details>


### [138] [PILOT in the Loop: Live Self-Improvement for Long-Horizon Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26530)
*Yang Xiao,Yusong Sun,Haoyi Wu,Wenyang Hui,Wen Da,Zhaokai Luo,Mu Chuan,Yao Hu,Wenjie Li,Chengyue Jiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PILOT是一个监督者-工作者架构的实时自改进框架，通过实时引导（live steering）和实时自进化（live self-evolution）两个耦合机制，在长时程智能体执行过程中即时重定向活跃运行并持续更新持久化技能库。在冻结骨干模型下，PILOT在五个基准配置中排名第一，显著提升任务成功率同时大幅降低输出token消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自改进方法通常在执行结束后才处理经验，无法重定向当前进行的运行，也无法即时应用和验证从中获得的经验教训。单智能体自校正将任务执行和轨迹评估混在一个上下文中，而子智能体委派虽分离了执行但无法重定向活跃子智能体。作者认为自改进应当是实时的（live），即利用涌现经验同时重定向活跃运行并更新持久化harness，但现有架构无法完全支持这一目标。

Method: PILOT采用监督者-工作者（supervisor-worker）架构，包含两个核心耦合机制：(1) 实时引导（live steering）：独立的监督者在执行过程中能够重定向或中止活跃的工作者；(2) 实时自进化（live self-evolution）：从执行过程中揭示的程序和失败模式中蒸馏出可复用的技能和记忆。

Result: 在冻结骨干模型和三个基准上，PILOT在六个配置中的五个排名第一。在Terminal-Bench 2.0上，PILOT比对照harness高出多达9.8个百分点。在自改进场景中，PILOT在GLM-5.1上提升14.6点，在Kimi-K2.6上提升12.4点。平均输出token分别下降42.9%和47.4%，每百万输出token的成功评估次数分别提升110.3%和134.0%。

Conclusion: 实时自改进架构PILOT证明，在执行过程中同时重定向活跃任务并进化持久化harness是可行且高效的，不仅提升了任务成功率，还大幅改善了token效率，为长时程智能体的自改进提供了新的设计范式。

Abstract: Long-horizon agent runs generate experience that can improve both the current run and future work. Most self-improvement methods process this experience only after execution ends, so they cannot redirect the active run or immediately apply and validate lessons learned from it. We argue that self-improvement should instead be live, using emerging experience both to redirect the active run and to update the persistent harness. Existing agent architectures do not fully support this goal. Single-agent self-correction combines task execution and trajectory assessment within one context, while subagent delegation separates execution but typically cannot redirect an active subagent. We present PILOT, a supervisor-worker harness for live self-improvement through two coupled mechanisms: (1) live steering lets a separate supervisor redirect or abort the active worker during execution; and (2) live self-evolution distils procedures and failure modes revealed during execution into reusable skills and memory. Across two frozen backbones and three benchmarks, PILOT ranks first in five of six configurations. On Terminal-Bench 2.0, PILOT outperforms counterpart harnesses by up to 9.8 percentage points. In the self-improvement setting, PILOT gains 14.6 points with GLM-5.1 and 12.4 points with Kimi-K2.6. Mean output tokens fall by 42.9% and 47.4%, while successful evaluations per million output tokens rise by 110.3% and 134.0%, respectively.

</details>


### [139] [Multi2AV-Safety: Benchmarking Safety in Multimodal-to-Audio-Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26535)
*Kaichao Jiang,Changtao Miao,Baiqi Wu,Zhiyuan Lu,Kang Yang,Peiwei Zhao,Junchi Chen,Yunfeng Diao,He Liu,Qi Chu,Tao Gong,Nenghai Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了Multi2AV-Safety，这是首个覆盖音频-视频生成中所有11种非单模态（文本/图像/音频/视频）条件组合配置的安全基准，包含11,024个攻击实例，揭示了现有多模态安全防护在组合风险感知方面的系统性缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 音频-视频生成正从提示驱动转向多模态条件驱动，有害意图可能不再存在于单一输入中，而是通过跨模态、跨时间的良性或弱有害条件交互涌现。现有安全基准大多以提示为中心或绑定固定条件接口，难以系统研究此类组合性风险。

Method: 构建Multi2AV-Safety基准，覆盖全部11种非单例T/I/A/V条件组合配置，包含11,024个攻击实例。攻击实例按照攻击机制和危害证据结构进行系统分类，并基于该基准对代表性多模态安全防护模型进行评估。

Result: 评估揭示了两种互补的失败模式：（1）由个体良性输入组合后涌现出有害语义；（2）显式有害线索与良性多模态上下文混合时更难被检测。两者共同表明现有多模态安全防护无法可靠地跨模态和跨时间整合安全证据，即使所有条件输入都可观测。

Conclusion: 识别出'组合性风险感知'（compositional risk perception）是多模态条件音频-视频生成安全防护的核心能力缺口，当前安全防护在此方面存在系统性失败。数据集预计于2026年10月公开发布。

Abstract: Audio-video generation is rapidly moving from prompt-driven synthesis toward multimodal conditioning, where text, images, audio, and video can jointly shape the generated output. This shift changes the nature of safety evaluation: harmful intent may no longer reside in any single input, but instead emerge from how otherwise benign or weakly harmful conditions interact across modalities and time. Existing safety benchmarks, however, remain largely prompt-centric or tied to fixed conditioning interfaces, leaving such compositional risks difficult to study systematically. To bridge this gap, we introduce Multi2AV-Safety, the first safety benchmark, to the best of our knowledge, to cover all 11 non-singleton T/I/A/V conditioning configurations for audio-video generation, comprising 11,024 attack instances. Evaluation on Multi2AV-Safety reveals systematic weaknesses in representative multimodal safety guards across attack mechanisms and harm-evidence structures. Our evaluation reveals two complementary failure modes: harmful semantics can emerge from the combination of individually benign inputs, while explicit harmful cues can become harder to detect when mixed with benign multimodal context. Together, these results identify \emph{compositional risk perception} as a central capability gap in safeguarding multimodal-conditioned audio-video generation: current safety guards fail to reliably integrate safety evidence across modalities and time, even when all conditioning inputs are observable. The dataset will be publicly released in October 2026.

</details>


### [140] [DuMateBench: Evaluating Autonomous Agents in Complex Real-World Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26546)
*Zechun Niu,Yukun Zhao,Jiaxin Zhang,Xu Shen,Jinhua Si,Han Tian,Can Xu,Yunfan Song,Jiaxin Mao,Yansong Gao,Yuchen Li,Jianmin Wu,Lingyong Yan,Shuaiqiang Wang,Dawei Yin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DuMateBench 是一个基于真实生产环境中匿名化用户会话重建的自主智能体基准测试，包含200个任务、8大场景和17个细粒度能力类别，并在隔离的Docker容器中注入三种真实环境复杂度（资源不足、不稳定、噪声）进行评测，采用混合确定性评估与LLM评判协议。实验表明现有智能体框架在严格任务完成上存在显著差距，性能受LLM能力与框架共同影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自主智能体基准通常按应用或能力分离任务，且在比实际场景更干净、更稳定的环境中评估，无法反映真实世界中的多工具工作流复杂性。为此需要一个基于真实会话、保留预解交互历史、持久配置和工作区状态的基准来更准确地衡量智能体在实际部署中的表现。

Method: 从大规模生产智能体平台收集匿名化并经过隐私筛选的用户会话，重建真实会话基准。每个任务保留预解交互历史、持久配置和工作区状态，并经人工验证。构建了覆盖200个任务、8大场景和17个细粒度能力类别的基准，多数任务需要多种能力协同。任务在注入三种环境复杂度（Insufficient、Unstable、Noisy）的隔离Docker容器中执行，采用混合确定性评估和LLM-as-Judge的评测协议。

Result: 对5个代表性自主智能体框架搭配4个先进LLM的实验显示，严格任务完成率存在显著差距。鲁棒性、效率和诊断分析进一步表明，环境扰动下的性能由LLM能力与智能体框架共同决定。

Conclusion: 现有自主智能体系统在真实环境下难以稳健完成复杂多工具任务，性能瓶颈由LLM与框架共同构成，DuMateBench可作为推动智能体在真实场景中鲁棒性提升的基准资源。

Abstract: Autonomous agents are increasingly adopted to complete complex, multi-tool workflows in real-world settings. However, existing benchmarks typically separate tasks by application or capability and evaluate agents in environments that are cleaner and more stable than those encountered in practice. We introduce DuMateBench, a real-session benchmark reconstructed from anonymized and privacy-screened user sessions collected from a large-scale production agent platform. Each task preserves the relevant pre-solution interaction history, persistent configurations, and workspace state, and is then validated through human verification. The resulting benchmark comprises 200 tasks spanning 8 broad scenarios and 17 fine-grained capability categories, with most tasks requiring multiple capability coordination. We execute these tasks in isolated Docker containers injected with three forms of real-world environmental complexity: Insufficient, Unstable, and Noisy, and assess performance using a hybrid deterministic and LLM-as-Judge evaluation protocol. Experiments across five representative autonomous-agent frameworks paired with four state-of-the-art LLMs reveal substantial gaps in strict task completion. Complementary robustness, efficiency, and diagnostic analyses further show that performance under environmental perturbations is jointly shaped by the capabilities of the LLM and the surrounding agent framework. The code and data are publicly available at https://dumatebench.com/.

</details>


### [141] [AgentJudgeBench: A Multi-Difficulty Benchmark for Evaluating LLM Judges on Agentic Tool-Calling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26623)
*Abhigya Verma,Amit Kumar Saha,Seganrasan Subramanian,Sai Harshitha Aluru*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AgentJudgeBench，首个系统研究LLM-as-a-judge在基于工作流DAG的智能体工具调用评估中可靠性的基准，包含3,808个实例、6种DAG拓扑和3个难度层级，使用5个生成器与6个评估器在有无ground-truth两种条件下进行评估。研究发现：评估器对齐度随任务难度单调下降，无ground-truth时下降快1.5倍；在困难任务且无ground-truth时，所有评估器收敛于77-82%的狭窄区间，暴露结构性天花板；ground-truth暴露并非普遍有益（对某些模型反而降低对齐，符合过度锚定效应）；链式思考推理和温度调节效果甚微，结构化评估量表可提升最多6.5pp的对齐度但泛化不均。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM评估器被广泛用于评估智能体工具调用系统，但它们在结构化、依赖驱动工作流上的可靠性尚未被系统研究。现有研究大多关注开放式文本或偏好评估，缺乏针对工作流DAG上LLM-as-a-judge可靠性的系统性评估。

Method: 构建包含3,808个实例的基准，覆盖6种DAG拓扑和3个难度层级；采用5个生成器（3B-70B开源模型及GPT-5.4）和6个评估器（20B到前沿规模）；在有无ground-truth两种配对条件下评估；并开展人类验证研究以及多种缓解策略（链式思考、温度调节、结构化量表）的对比实验。

Result: 评估器对齐度随任务难度单调下降，且无ground-truth时下降速度加快1.5倍；困难任务无ground-truth场景下所有评估器收敛于77-82%的狭窄区间，表明存在主要由任务难度决定的结构性天花板；ground-truth对GPT-5.4（下降1.5pp）和Gemini-2.5-Pro（下降3.9pp）反而降低对齐（过度锚定）；链式思考和温度几乎无效，结构化评价量表最多提升6.5pp但泛化不均；有ground-truth时QwQ-32B最优，人类验证显示GPT-OSS-120B最符合人类判断；无ground-truth时前沿评估器仅在共享天花板内略有领先。

Conclusion: 当前LLM评估器在智能体工具调用工作流评估中存在根本性局限性，任务难度是限制评估质量的主要因素而非模型规模。研究为智能体系统的可靠评估提供了实用指导：应谨慎使用ground-truth（存在过度锚定风险）、结构化评估量表是更有效的缓解手段、单纯增大模型规模或使用链式思考无法突破结构性天花板。

Abstract: LLM judges are widely used to evaluate agentic tool-calling systems, yet their reliability on structured, dependency-driven workflows remains largely unexamined. We present AgentJudgeBench, the first benchmark to systematically study LLM-as-a-judge reliability for agentic tool-calling over workflow DAGs, as distinct from the broader LLM-as-a-judge task of open-ended text or preference evaluation. The benchmark comprises 3,808 instances spanning six DAG topologies and three difficulty tiers, evaluated with five generators (3B-70B open-weight models and GPT-5.4) and six judges (20B to frontier scale) under paired with- and without-ground-truth conditions. Judge alignment degrades monotonically with task difficulty, 1.5x faster without ground truth, and on hard queries without ground truth all six judges converge to a narrow 77-82% band regardless of scale, revealing a structural ceiling driven primarily by task difficulty, though its height is partly prompt-dependent for weaker generators, that model capacity alone cannot overcome. Ground-truth exposure is not uniformly beneficial: it reduces alignment for GPT-5.4 (1.5 pp) and Gemini-2.5-Pro (3.9 pp), consistent with over-anchoring. Among mitigation strategies, chain-of-thought reasoning and judge temperature both have negligible effect, while structured evaluation rubrics improve alignment by up to 6.5 pp but do not generalize uniformly across judge-generator pairs. With ground truth, QwQ-32B best matches the programmatic reference, while a human validation study identifies GPT-OSS-120B as the most human-aligned judge; without it, frontier judges lead only marginally within the shared ceiling. These results expose fundamental limitations of current LLM judges and yield practical guidelines for reliable evaluation in agentic systems.

</details>


### [142] [SIGMA: Structured Noise-Effect-Aware Grouped Multi-Agent Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26683)
*Li Mingqian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SIGMA，一个利用协作结构学习鲁棒表示的分层多智能体强化学习框架，用于应对噪声观测下协调的鲁棒性问题。通过密度分组的自适应局部结构和组内共识聚合、组间注意力机制，SIGMA在保留下游任务相关信息的同时平滑噪声引起的表示偏差，在星际争霸II噪声观测任务中验证了结构化噪声效应并提升了鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协作式多智能体强化学习在噪声观测下维持鲁棒协调面临重大挑战。虽然观测扰动通常独立引入，但其对协作决策的下游影响通过底层协作结构变得结构化——即"结构化噪声效应"。现有鲁棒MARL方法很少显式刻画或利用这种结构依赖的噪声效应，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: SIGMA采用分层协作框架：(1) 通过基于密度的分组将智能体组织成自适应的局部结构；(2) 执行组内共识聚合，保留共享任务相关信息同时平滑智能体特定的表示偏差；(3) 通过组间注意力机制自适应地整合不同组的信息，在保留全局协调的同时容纳各组异质贡献。

Result: 在星际争霸II的噪声观测任务上，实验经验性地验证了结构化噪声效应的存在，并证明SIGMA在观测噪声下持续提升鲁棒性，同时在无噪声环境中保持有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: SIGMA通过显式利用协作结构来学习噪声下的鲁棒表示，证明了结构感知的鲁棒策略在MARL中的有效性，为处理结构化噪声效应提供了一种新的分层协作范式。

Abstract: Cooperative multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) faces significant challenges in maintaining robust coordination under noisy observations. Although observation disturbances are often introduced independently across agents, their downstream effects on cooperative decision-making can become structured through underlying cooperation structures. We characterize this phenomenon as structured noise effects, where noise-induced decision effects exhibit local correlation among agents with stronger task-related dependencies while remaining globally heterogeneous across different agents and local structures. Existing robust MARL methods, however, rarely explicitly characterize or exploit such structure-dependent noise effects. To address this limitation, we propose SIGMA, a hierarchical collaboration framework that exploits cooperation structures to learn robust representations under noisy observations. SIGMA first organizes agents into adaptive local structures through density-based grouping and performs intra-group consensus aggregation to preserve shared task-relevant information while smoothing agent-specific representation deviations. Inter-group attention then adaptively integrates information across different groups to preserve global coordination while accommodating their heterogeneous contributions. Experiments on noisy-observation tasks in StarCraft II empirically validate the structured noise effects and demonstrate that SIGMA consistently improves robustness under observation noise while maintaining competitive performance in noise-free environments.

</details>


### [143] [Relational Over-Regularization: Graph-Based AI-Generated Text Detection via Sentence Transition Deviation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26694)
*Hyeonchu Park,Bugeun Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出关系过度正则化（ROR）这一句对级结构信号用于检测AI生成文本，并给出CSFG图框架作为具体实现，在四个基准上取得97.14%准确率与良好泛化性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AIGT检测方法依赖词元级统计信号或独立风格特征，容易过拟合特定生成器并在分布偏移下失效，需要更鲁棒的结构性信号。

Method: 识别LLM在句间转换方差上偏离人类写作的信号（ROR），将其建模为边特征，通过CSFG图框架编码位置、顺序、语义及转换偏差信号，用可学习的GNN边特征来实现，并以每边带符号偏差δ_ij作为无阈值校准器。

Result: CSFG在二分类检测中达到97.14%准确率，比最强图基线高11.14个百分点，误报率1.57%；在方差膨胀的生成器上对未见过的LLM泛化稳健，但对转换方差等于或低于人类基线的生成器检测性能下降。

Conclusion: 核心贡献是关系层面问题建模（ROR）而非新颖的GNN架构；该结构信号为AIGT检测提供了在分布偏移下更稳健的新方向，但其适用范围受限于生成器方差膨胀的假设。

Abstract: Detecting AI-generated text (AIGT) remains challenging because existing approaches rely on token-level statistical signals or independent stylometric features, causing them to overfit to specific generators and fail under distribution shift. We identify a structural signal at the sentence-pair level: LLMs produce inter-sentence transition variance that deviates from human writing through inflated variance driven by recurring similarity bursts at paragraph boundaries and templated transitions. We formalize this as Relational Over-Regularization (ROR) and validate it across four benchmarks (p < 0.001). The central contribution is this relational problem formulation, not a novel GNN architecture; CSFG is one concrete instantiation for operationalizing ROR. To exploit this signal, we propose the Cross-Source Stylometric Fingerprint Graph (CSFG), a graph-based framework that encodes positional, sequential, semantic, and transition deviation signals as learnable GNN edge features. The per-edge signed deviation δ_ij operationalizes ROR without hand-crafted thresholds and acts as a false-positive calibrator. CSFG achieves 97.14% accuracy under binary detection, outperforming the strongest graph-based baseline by 11.14 pp, with a false-positive rate of 1.57% and robust generalization to unseen LLMs in the inflated-variance regime; detection degrades for generators whose transition variance falls at or below the human baseline.

</details>


### [144] [Five Primitives for Governing Autonomous AI Agents at Runtime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26696)
*Jiten Oswal,John Cadeddu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出企业级自主AI代理的运行治理是一个运行时问题而非模型对齐或构建时问题，提出了五个核心原语（发现、身份、治理、证明、供应链），并描述了一个将代理动作在生效前进行策略检查、按租户动作词汇表授权并记录到哈希链签名账本的实现方案，其中四个原语已在私有试点运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业部署自主AI代理时沿用了为人类用户和长期服务设计的控制模型，但在三个关键方面失效：代理主体是短暂且快速涌现的（比资源供给快）；代理动作由模型而非程序选择，行为集不可预知；代理可被任何人通过API创建，种群是被发现而非被供给的。因此需要一个专为自主代理设计的运行时治理框架。

Method: 论文从"动作生效前和生效后必须回答的问题"推导出五个治理原语：发现（discovery）、身份（identity）、治理（governance）、证明（attestation）和供应链（supply chain）。对每个原语说明了缺失时导致的失败以及为何其他原语无法结构性替代。实现中，代理动作在生效前对照策略进行中介检查，按租户动作词汇表授权，并记录在可被第三方在厂商不在场情况下验证的哈希链接签名账本中。

Result: 架构有明确的成本：执行点位于请求关键路径上，身份需要每个工作负载附带sidecar，fail-closed中介将可用性事件转化为拒绝。实现状态明确：四个原语已在私有试点中构建并运行，第五个（供应链）作为独立工具构建但尚未集成到请求路径中。作者刻意保留五部分分解，避免将其简化为对已构建代码库的描述而非真正的分类体系。

Conclusion: 自主AI代理的治理应被定位为运行时问题，需要专门的原语来应对代理短暂性、行为不可预测性和种群动态发现性。五原语分解提供了一个结构性框架，但实现成本和集成完整性需要明确承认，且分类体系应当超越单一代码库的实现描述。

Abstract: Enterprise deployments of autonomous AI agents inherit a control model built for human users and long-lived services, and the fit fails in three specific ways: agent principals are ephemeral, appearing and vanishing faster than provisioning; their actions are selected by a model rather than programmed, so the set of things they may attempt is not known in advance; and the population is discovered rather than provisioned, because anyone who can call an API can create one. We argue that governing such agents is a runtime problem -- not a model-alignment problem and not a build-time problem -- and we derive five primitives from the questions that must be answered before an action takes effect and after it has: discovery, identity, governance, attestation, and supply chain. For each we state what fails if it is absent and why the others cannot structurally supply it. We describe an implementation in which an agent's action is mediated against policy before it takes effect, authorised against a per-tenant action vocabulary, and recorded in a hash-linked signed ledger a third party can verify with the vendor out of the loop. We report what the architecture costs: the enforcement point sits on the request's critical path, identity requires a sidecar per workload, and fail-closed mediation converts availability incidents into denial. We are explicit about implementation status: four primitives are built and running in private pilots, and the fifth is built as separate tooling and not yet integrated into the request path. We keep it in the set deliberately: a five-part decomposition that exactly matches what its authors happened to build is not a taxonomy but a description of a codebase.

</details>


### [145] [Accelerating Scientific Research with Gemini in the Real-World](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26701)
*Samuel Schmidgall,Xiaokai Zhu,Marian Shaw,Lin Yang,Valentin Liévin,Jingyun Yang,Yuchen Zhuang,Tim Strother,Alex Bijamov,Min Woo Sun,Anil Palepu,Justin Chen,David Steiner,Jacqueline Shreibati,Wei-Hung Weng,Yilin Zhao,Xingjian Hu,Nicholas Zahn,Sadhya Garg,Julia Kirby,Yuxiang Gan,Jiaoli Li,Divy Thakkar,Shekoofeh Azizi,David Racz,Juraj Gottweis,Vivek Natarajan,Chenglin Wu,Tal Danino,Keran Rong,Haozhe Wang,Benoit Schillings,Yong Cheng,Quoc V. Le,Tao Tu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Co-Scientist系统（基于Gemini的多智能体系统）从模拟假设生成扩展为执行落地的研究伙伴，在材料科学、生物学和计算机科学三大领域实现了闭环科研流程：在材料科学中与CVD反应堆接口设计了MXene前驱体路线并成功生长2D层状材料及单层MoS2/MoSe2/WS2半导体；在生物学中从稀疏成像数据定量预测了工程大肠杆菌的群体泳动表型；在计算机科学中自主发现了超越六个前沿模型的推理时缩放架构。30位领域专家的450项双盲评审显示其可靠性模块降低了幻觉和剽窃，加强了研究安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推动多智能体AI系统从纯计算（in silico）假设生成向真实实验执行闭环的方向发展，弥合AI辅助科研中理论生成与实验落地之间的鸿沟，实现真正加速真实世界科学发现的目标。

Method: 将Co-Scientist（基于Gemini的多智能体框架）扩展为执行导向的科研系统，使其能够与实验设备接口（如半自动CVD反应堆）实现实验循环中的快速迭代调配；利用Gemini 3 Deep Think进行快速的实验室闭环执行；针对不同学科（材料、生物、计算机科学）定制工作流程；引入可靠性模块（反幻觉和反剽窃机制），并通过30名专家、450项评审的双盲研究验证生成论文质量。

Result: 材料科学方面：设计了MXene的安全生产前驱体路线并获得了与Ti3C2Tx MXene晶格结构相似的层状2D材料；在数分钟内定制生长配方，实现单次尝试生长单层MoS2、MoSe2和WS2。生物学方面：定量预测了工程大肠杆菌跨IPTG梯度的群体泳动表型，与未发表的湿实验室形态学测量定量吻合。计算机科学方面：自主发现推理时缩放架构，在HealthBench（Hard和Professional）上超越六个前沿模型，并在盲法医生评估中减少潜在临床危害。可靠性方面：双盲研究证实可靠性模块减少幻觉和剽窃。

Conclusion: Co-Scientist展示了多智能体科学AI系统在跨学科真实实验闭环中的可行性和有效性，标志着AI从假设生成器向执行落地的科研合作者迈进，为未来闭环自主科学发现系统奠定了实证基础。

Abstract: We present an extension and comprehensive real-world validation of Co-Scientist, a Gemini-based multi-agent system designed to accelerate end-to-end scientific research across hypothesis generation, experimentation, and manuscript generation. Moving beyond in silico hypothesis generation, this specialized configuration transitions Co-Scientist into an execution-grounded research partner advancing closed-loop scientific workflows across materials science, biology, and computer science. In materials science, Co-Scientist interfaced with a semi-automated chemical vapor deposition reactor to design a safe precursor route for MXenes; experimental execution produced a lamellar 2D material sharing key structural similarities with the Ti3C2Tx MXene lattice, although further experiments are needed to confirm the atomic structure. Leveraging Gemini 3 Deep Think for rapid, lab-in-the-loop execution, it also tailored growth recipes to laboratory constraints in minutes, enabling single-attempt growth of monolayer MoS2, MoSe2, and WS2 semiconductors. In biology, Co-Scientist predicted emergent swarming phenotypes of engineered E. coli across inducer (IPTG) gradients from sparse imaging data, quantitatively matching unpublished wet-lab morphological measurements. In computer science, Co-Scientist autonomously discovered an inference-time scaling architecture that outperformed six frontier models on HealthBench (Hard and Professional) while reducing potential clinical harm under blinded physician evaluation. Finally, a double-blind study of end-to-end generated papers with 30 domain experts across 450 reviews demonstrates that Co-Scientist's reliability modules reduce hallucination and plagiarism while improving research safety. Together, these results demonstrate progress toward closed-loop multi-agent scientific AI systems capable of accelerating real-world scientific discovery.

</details>


### [146] [Style as a Confound: False Positives in AI Detection of Non-Native Academic Writing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26710)
*Hyeonchu Park,Gahye Jeong,Bugeun Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究基于135,389对专业编辑前后的手稿，系统评估了13种AI文本检测器，发现编辑行为本身会显著影响检测器输出（改变AI分数），且不同检测器对同一编辑的反应方向和幅度存在巨大差异，表明专业编辑风格是AI检测器输出的关键混淆变量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI文本检测器在学术环境中被广泛应用，但其输出是否真正反映AI作者身份，还是只是反映与"润色后的学术英语"相关的语言特征，尚不清楚。以往研究虽报告了非英语母语写作的高误报率，但群体统计混淆了作者身份与主题、领域和写作风格差异。专业编辑场景提供了控制作者身份和内容的理想实验条件。

Method: 利用一家专业英语编辑服务（2018-2025）的135,389对文档，每对包含非英语母语作者原稿及其经过母语编辑的版本，在保持作者和内容不变的前提下，比较13种AI文本检测器对编辑前后文档的响应变化。

Result: (1) 13种检测器对人类书写文本的误报率差异极大，从0.0%到100.0%；(2) 不同检测器对相同编辑的反应方向不一致，有些检测器AI分数升高，有些则降低；(3) 分数变化与编辑程度相关。

Conclusion: 专业编辑风格是AI检测器输出的关键混淆变量，而非实现了文本来源与语言风格的完全分离。这引发了对检测器在学术环境中公平性和可靠性的严重担忧。

Abstract: AI text detectors are increasingly employed in academic settings, but it remains unclear whether their outputs reflect AI authorship itself or broader linguistic features associated with polished academic English. Previous studies have reported high false-positive rates (FPRs) for non-native English writing, but population-level comparisons confound authorship with differences in topic, domain, and writing style. Professional editing provides a useful setting for examining this issue because it changes the linguistic form of manuscripts while preserving authorship and content. We examined 135,389 document pairs from a professional English editing service (2018-2025), comprising non-native manuscripts and their native-edited versions, to assess how editing affects detector responses controlling for content and authorship. For the 13 AI text detectors, FPRs for human-written texts varied widely, from 0.0% to 100.0%. Responses varied across detectors: the same edits increased AI scores in some detectors but decreased them in others. Notably, score changes correlated with the extent of editing. The findings identify professional editing style as a key confounding variable in AI detector outputs, rather than establishing a full separation of text origin from linguistic style, raising concerns about fairness and reliability in academic settings.

</details>


### [147] [Knowing When Not to Reuse: Conditional Experience Transfer in Autonomous LLM Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26730)
*Tingyun Li,Wenfeng Feng,Weiqing Li,Abudukelimu Wuerkaixi,Guohua Liu,Yuewei Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出边界校准干预迁移（BCIT）方法，用于解决自主后训练中经验迁移的条件性问题。BCIT在权重变更训练前对历史更新证据进行条件化授权，绑定效果与来源上下文，检查适用条件，否决冲突候选，并在必要时通过有界训练试验获取当前状态证据。在4B模型上的金融推理、文本转SQL和函数调用任务中，BCIT相比基线授权更少有害更新，并在同等算力预算下获得更高最终模型质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型适应不断演化的领域、工具和需求时往往需要重复后训练，自主系统通过提议更新、训练候选和评估反馈来自动化该过程。但随着证据积累，一个核心问题浮出水面：在父模型经过后续训练改变后，哪些过去的更新证据仍然可执行？更新效果取决于其父模型、数据和训练阶段，将过去的成功视为无条件许可会浪费计算资源，且若子模型被提升还可能退化后续训练轨迹。

Method: 本文将该问题形式化为"条件经验迁移"，并提出边界校准干预迁移（BCIT）方法。BCIT在权重变更训练之前授权经验重用，具体包括：将观察到的效果绑定到其来源上下文；检查适用条件是否满足；否决具有命名的硬冲突的候选；在需要时通过有界训练试验获取当前状态证据。完全训练的候选仍需遵循共享采纳规则，且只有观察到的事件才能扩展记忆。

Result: 在4B模型上的金融推理、文本转SQL和函数调用三个领域实验中，候选更新在评估上下文中表现出异构的目标与保留效果。在匹配候选、证据和计算资源的条件下，BCIT相比评估的替代方法授权更少有害更新，并在相同算力预算下获得更高的最终模型质量。

Conclusion: 实验结果支持将"经验授权"视为自主后训练中的一个独立问题的观点。BCIT通过条件化地授权历史证据重用，避免上下文无关的盲目许可，从而在有限计算预算下实现更优的模型质量。

Abstract: Large language models offer broad capabilities, but adapting them to evolving domains, tools, and requirements often entails repeated post-training. Autonomous systems automate parts of this process by proposing updates, training candidates, and using evaluation feedback to select subsequent proposals. As evidence accumulates, a central problem emerges: which past update evidence remains actionable after subsequent training has changed the parent model? An update's effect depends on its parent, data, and training stage. Treating past success as context-free permission can waste compute. If the resulting child is promoted, it can also degrade the subsequent training trajectory. We formulate this problem as conditional experience transfer and introduce Boundary-Calibrated Intervention Transfer (BCIT), a method that authorizes experience reuse before weight-changing training. BCIT binds an observed effect to its source context, checks applicability conditions, vetoes candidates with named hard conflicts, and obtains current-state evidence through a bounded training trial when needed. Fully trained candidates still face a shared adoption rule, and only observed events extend memory. On one 4B model adapted across finance reasoning, text-to-SQL, and function calling, candidate updates exhibit heterogeneous target and retention effects across the evaluated contexts. Under matched candidates, evidence, and compute, BCIT authorizes fewer harmful updates and attains higher equal-budget final-model quality than the evaluated alternatives. These results support treating experience authorization as a distinct problem in autonomous post-training.

</details>


### [148] [Graph-Guided Selective Unlearning for Language Models: Controlling Support Routes Beyond Forget Seeds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26743)
*Waqas Khan,Tabinda Sarwar,Jingyue Cong,Xun Yi,Estrid He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出GRAPHSU，一种图引导的控制器，通过构建加权支持路由图来扩展机器遗忘的删除范围，超越显式遗忘种子，对高风险邻居施加分级遗忘强度，在TOFU和PISTOL基准上相比仅种子基线降低了最多49.5个百分点的泄漏率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业微调语言模型后，因隐私、合同或合规要求可能需要删除某些知识。现有选择性遗忘方法仅将显式标注的遗忘样本作为完整删除范围，这不够充分——目标知识仍可通过改写、别名或邻近训练样本被恢复。

Method: GRAPHSU构建一个加权支持-路由图，通过图中传播删除压力，对高风险邻居施加分级遗忘强度，从而将删除范围从遗忘种子扩展到更广的相关样本集合。

Result: 在TOFU和PISTOL基准上，使用GPT-2 Medium和Llama-3.2-3B-Instruct，GRAPHSU在所有删除设置下实现了最低的效用-可行软泄漏率，相比匹配的仅种子基线最多降低49.5个百分点。

Conclusion: 有效的企业级遗忘需要控制支持路由而非仅仅遗忘种子，GRAPHSU证明图引导的删除范围扩展能显著提升遗忘效果并保持模型效用。

Abstract: Enterprises fine-tune language models on proprietary data that may later require removal due to privacy, contractual, or compliance obligations. Selective unlearning removes requested knowledge while preserving model utility, offering a practical alternative to full retraining, but existing methods treat the explicitly identified forget examples as the complete deletion scope. This is insufficient when target knowledge remains recoverable through paraphrases, aliases, or neighboring training examples. We propose GRAPHSU, a graph-guided controller that expands the deletion scope beyond forget seeds by constructing a weighted support-route graph, propagating deletion pressure through it, and applying graded forgetting strengths to high-risk neighbors. On the Task of Fictitious Unlearning (TOFU), a synthetic author-profile question-answering benchmark, and PISTOL, a structural-unlearning benchmark built around interconnected factual samples, with GPT-2 Medium and Llama-3.2-3B-Instruct, GRAPHSU achieves the lowest utility-feasible soft leakage across all deletion settings, reducing leakage by up to 49.5 percentage points over a matched seed-only baseline, demonstrating that effective enterprise unlearning requires controlling support routes, not just forget seeds.

</details>


### [149] [AgentFold: Closed-Loop Agentic Search for Protein Folding Model Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26747)
*Mingquan Liu,Jiangyu Chen,Hanqun Cao,Xujun Zhang,Pengsen Ma,Xiangru Tang,Shuting Jin,Zhuo Yang,Tianfan Fu,Fang Wu,Xiangxiang Zeng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AgentFold是一个多智能体框架，将蛋白质折叠模型开发形式化为对可执行代码变体的闭环搜索，在匹配计算预算下将最佳lDDT较独立Codex方案提升7.5%，并揭示了稳定的经验设计模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前科学LLM智能体在文献推理、工具使用和实验规划方面已有潜力，但尚不清楚它们能否通过对可执行代码修改和计算昂贵的验证来自主改进大型、紧耦合的科学机器学习系统。蛋白质折叠领域尤其需要协调架构修改、多目标评估和领域感知解释，是检验这一能力的理想场景。

Method: AgentFold采用多智能体框架，以ESMFold为起点，将折叠模型开发构建为闭环搜索：(1)提出假设；(2)实现并调试代码级修改；(3)评估模型变体；(4)分析实验结果；(5)将成功与失败的干预存入结构化记忆。同时采用类似蒙特卡洛树搜索(MCTS)的策略，将计算资源分配给高分搜索分支。

Result: 在超过2000行代码的工程级蛋白质折叠代码库上，AgentFold探索了约80个模型变体，消耗约5000 GPU小时和1.7亿LLM token。在匹配计算预算下，AgentFold较独立Codex提议将最佳lDDT提升7.5%，并优于随机搜索对照组。干预轨迹揭示：稳定的收益通常源于早期、软性、可学习的先验和门控精炼，而直接的几何扰动和几何条件反馈常破坏训练稳定性。

Conclusion: AgentFold证明了LLM智能体能够自主改善大型紧耦合科学机器学习系统，不仅带来了模型性能提升，还通过干预轨迹提供了可复用的经验设计模式，为科学自动化研发提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Scientific LLM agents have shown promise in literature reasoning, tool use, and experiment planning, but it remains unclear whether they can autonomously improve large, tightly coupled scientific machine-learning systems through executable code changes and computationally expensive validation. We study this question in protein folding, where progress requires coordinated architectural modifications, multi-objective evaluation, and domain-aware interpretation. We present AgentFold, a multi-agent framework that formulates folding-model development as a closed-loop search over executable code variants. Starting from ESMFold, AgentFold proposes hypotheses, implements and debugs code-level modifications, evaluates model variants, analyzes experimental outcomes, and stores both successful and failed interventions in structured memory. An MCTS-style policy allocates computational resources across high-scoring search branches. On an engineering-scale protein-folding codebase comprising more than 2,000 lines of code, AgentFold explores approximately 80 model variants using approximately 5,000 GPU-hours and 170 million LLM tokens. Under a matched computational budget, AgentFold improves the best lDDT by 7.5% over independent Codex proposals and outperforms a random-search control. Beyond model improvement, the resulting intervention traces reveal recurring empirical design patterns: stable gains tend to arise from early, soft, learnable priors and gated refinement, whereas direct geometric perturbations and geometry-conditioned feedback often destabilize training. The code and experimental resources are publicly available at https://github.com/lmqfly/AgentFold.

</details>


### [150] [Discovering Relationships in Data Lakes Using Large Language Models: An Industrial Case](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26750)
*Ahlame Diouan,Eric Ferey,Sabine Loudcher,Jérôme Darmont*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ColRel提出了一种两阶段方法，利用数据湖摄取时可用的元数据和数据构建列嵌入，用于列关系发现。在编码或缩写模式标签（如ERP数据集）等困难场景下，借助业务词典更好地解释列名并生成短自然语言描述。在公共基准和工业ERP数据集上的实验表明，ColRel在语义相关、弱信号场景中尤为有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据湖依赖元数据保持可用性，但元数据往往有限或信息量不足，尤其在ERP衍生的数据集中，列名采用编码或缩写模式标签，导致列关系发现困难。

Method: ColRel是一个两阶段方法：第一阶段从摄取时可用的元数据和数据构建列嵌入；第二阶段中，针对编码模式等困难情况，利用业务词典更好解释列名，并生成简短的列自然语言描述用于嵌入构建。

Result: 在公共基准测试和工业ERP数据集上的实验表明，ColRel在语义相关、弱信号设置下表现尤为有效。

Conclusion: ColRel证明了在元数据稀疏或编码化的数据集上，结合元数据、数据内容及业务词典的两阶段方法能显著提升列关系发现的性能，尤其适用于语义相关但信号微弱的场景。

Abstract: Data lakes rely on metadata to remain usable, yet this meta data is often limited or weakly informative for column relationship discovery, especially in ERP-derived datasets with coded or abbreviated schema labels. We propose ColRel, a two-stage method that builds column embeddings from metadata and data available at ingestion time. In difficult cases, such as coded schemata, business dictionaries help better interpret column names and support the generation of short natural-language descriptions used in the second stage. Experiments on public benchmarks and an industrial ERP dataset show that ColRel is particularly effective in semantically related, weak-signal settings.

</details>


### [151] [DEEPCHART: How Far are LLMs from Faithful Data-Science Chart Generation?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26757)
*Jiahui tang,Kuicai Dong,Dexun Li,Hongchao Gu,Haocheng Yu,Wei Han,Chen Zhang,Yong Liu,Hao Wang,Enhong Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DEEPCHART基准，包含1,482个来自真实科学论文、财务报告和生态报告的任务条件图表生成实例，将图表生成形式化为"提取-推理-可视化"流水线并分阶段评估。实验发现视觉上合理的图表常掩盖数据级幻觉，且越大上下文窗口并不足以解决该问题，还需要可靠的证据提取和定量推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代LLM虽能生成视觉上合理、符合指令的图表，但在长且嘈杂的多模态上下文中，数据级幻觉难以检测。现有评估无法衡量"视觉合理但数据错误"这一关键差距，需要专门基准来量化忠实图表生成的缺口。

Method: 构建DEEPCHART基准，包含1,482个专家标注的任务条件图表生成实例，来源涵盖科学论文、财务申报和生态系统报告。将图表生成形式化为Extract-Reason-Visualize流水线，分别在源数据提取、派生数据推理和图表渲染三个阶段进行逐阶段评估。

Result: 对最先进模型的实验表明，视觉上合理的图表往往掩盖数据级幻觉；在真实长文本和多模态场景中，提取和推理错误普遍存在。

Conclusion: 单纯扩大上下文窗口不足以实现忠实图表生成，可靠的证据提取和定量推理在渲染前同样至关重要。相关工作与资源已公开。

Abstract: Faithful chart generation in real-world data-science workflows requires grounding visualizations in scattered evidence, computing chart-ready quantities, and rendering them accurately. Modern LLMs can produce visually plausible, instruction-compliant charts, yet data-level hallucinations remain difficult to detect in long, noisy, and multimodal contexts. To measure this gap, we introduce DEEPCHART, an expert-annotated benchmark of 1,482 task-conditioned chart-generation instances drawn from real-world scientific papers, financial filings, and ecosystem reports. DEEPCHART formulates chart generation as an Extract--Reason--Visualize pipeline and evaluates source-data extraction, derived-data reasoning, and chart rendering stage by stage. Experiments with state-of-the-art models show that visually plausible charts often conceal data-level hallucinations, with extraction and reasoning errors common in realistic long and multimodal settings. These findings suggest that larger context windows alone are insufficient; faithful chart generation also requires reliable evidence extraction and quantitative reasoning before rendering. Our benchmark and associated resources are available at https://github.com/tangdouer1005/DeepChart.

</details>


### [152] [Categorizer Automata for Discounted-Sum Payoffs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26763)
*Nathalie Bertrand,Pranav Ghorpade,Senthil Rajasekaran,Sasha Rubin,Moshe Vardi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"分类器自动机"（categorizer automaton），用于将无限奖励序列的折现和分类到有限数量的箱中，状态空间与箱数成线性关系（优于比较器自动机叉积的指数复杂度）。将其应用于马尔可夫决策过程，可综合最大化折现和效用的策略，支持不连续效用函数；对于分段常数效用精确且伪多项式时间，对于分段Lipschitz效用得到ε-最优策略且同为伪多项式时间，并证明了该综合问题的PSPACE难性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将连续数据离散化为箱是AI中的基本操作。已有比较器自动机（两箱特例）在定量综合中已被证明有用，但通过比较器自动机叉积构造多箱分类器时状态空间呈指数增长。本文旨在构造状态空间与箱数线性相关的分类器自动机，以支持更高效的多类分类，并将之应用于MDP中不连续效用函数的策略综合。

Method: 1) 引入分类器自动机，定义其读取无限奖励序列并确定折现和所在箱；2) 构造状态空间为O(箱数)的决定性自动机，替代比较器自动机叉积的指数构造；3) 将分类器自动机嵌入马尔可夫决策过程，针对分段常数效用给出精确算法（伪多项式时间），针对分段Lipschitz效用给出ε-最优策略算法（伪多项式时间）；4) 通过归约证明分段常数效用下的综合问题为PSPACE-hard。

Result: 构造出状态空间与箱数线性关系的分类器自动机；在MDP中，分段常数效用下综合算法精确且伪多项式时间，分段Lipschitz效用下得到ε-最优策略且同样伪多项式时间；同时证明该综合问题在分段常数效用下即为PSPACE-hard。

Conclusion: 分类器自动机是比对标器自动机更一般的工具，以线性状态空间支持多箱折现和分类，可高效解决MDP中不连续效用函数（分段常数和分段Lipschitz）下的策略综合问题，且算法在伪多项式时间内完成。该问题本身的PSPACE-hard下界表明在一般情形下不存在多项式时间精确算法。

Abstract: Categorizing continuous data into discrete bins is a fundamental operation in artificial intelligence. We introduce the categorizer automaton, a deterministic automaton that reads an infinite sequence of rewards and identifies which of finitely many bins contains its discounted sum. Categorizer automata generalize comparator automata, the special case of two bins, which have already proven useful in quantitative synthesis. Our main technical contribution is the construction of a categorizer automaton whose state space is linear in the number of bins, rather than exponential as obtained by a cross-product of comparator automata. We then apply categorizer automata to Markov decision processes, where they allow one to synthesize policies that maximize the expected utility of a discounted-sum payoff for utility functions that may be discontinuous. For piecewise-constant utility functions, the resulting algorithm is exact and runs in pseudo-polynomial time. For piecewise-Lipschitz utility functions, a class that includes any utility with bounded slope between finitely many jumps, it again runs in pseudo-polynomial time and yields an $\varepsilon$-optimal policy. We also show that the synthesis problem considered is PSPACE-hard already for piecewise-constant utilities.

</details>


### [153] [AI Control Scientist: LLM-driven Agentic System for Automated Control Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26780)
*Haiteng Wang,Weihao Li,Jing Zhang,Lei Ren*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AI Control Scientist (AICS)，一个由大语言模型驱动的智能体系统，能够从语言设计需求自动生成优化的控制系统，通过任务建模、控制器设计和参数调优三个智能体协同工作，在多个代表性控制系统的设计成功率和优化效率上优于现有自动化基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统控制系统设计依赖专家知识和大量手动参数调优，效率和可扩展性受限。本文旨在利用大语言模型驱动智能体，将控制系统设计从人工驱动转变为智能体驱动，以提升设计效率和自动化程度。

Method: 提出AICS框架，包含三个核心智能体：(1) 任务建模智能体将用户语言需求解析为工程约束；(2) 控制器设计智能体生成候选控制器结构和可执行代码；(3) 参数调优智能体在闭环性能准则下细化控制器参数。

Result: 实验表明AICS能够自动生成多个代表性控制系统，在设计成功率和优化效率方面均优于现有自动化基线方法。

Conclusion: 该工作有望将控制系统设计从人工驱动转变为智能体驱动，为模型预测控制及其他先进控制系统的设计铺平道路。

Abstract: Control system design is critical for modern industry, such as chemical process temperature regulation and aero-engine control. However,traditional control design workflows rely heavily on expert knowledge and extensive manual parameter tuning, resulting in limited efficiency and scalability. To this end, this paper proposes AI Control Scientist (AICS), the first large language model (LLM)-driven agent capable of automatically generating optimized controller from language design requirements. Specifically, a Task Modeling Agent interprets user requirements to engineering constraints; a Controller Design Agent generate candidate controller structures and executable code; and a Parameter Tuning Agent refine controller parameters under closed-loop performance criteria. Experiments demonstrate that the proposed agentic system can automatically generate multiple representative control systems, outperforms existing automated baselines in both design success rate and optimization efficiency. This work has the potential to transform control system design from human-driven to agent-driven, paving the way for model predictive control and other advanced control systems design.

</details>


### [154] [SymbolLKG: Towards Verifiable Logical Reasoning via Logical Knowledge Graph and Symbolic Solvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26836)
*Haizhao Fan,Yuchi Xiong,Jize Wang,Xinping Guan,Xinyi Le*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种神经符号架构，将逻辑知识图谱（LKG）与动态求解器路由相结合，以解决LLM在严格多步推理中的幻觉和不一致问题。通过将逻辑规则和约束作为一等拓扑节点显式建模，并设计逻辑路由器将任务动态分派到最优符号引擎，在逻辑推理基准上显著超越SOTA提示方法和RAG基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM虽在自然语言理解上表现优异，但在严格多步推理中常出现幻觉和不一致；现有CoT方法缺乏严格验证机制，标准RAG又难以捕捉逻辑任务中复杂的结构依赖关系。

Method: 提出Neuro-Symbolic架构，包含三个关键组件：（1）基于本体论的逻辑知识图谱（LKG），将逻辑规则和约束作为一等拓扑节点，显式建模从文本中提取的依赖关系；（2）Logic Router，动态将任务分派到最优符号推理引擎；（3）拓扑感知的混合检索机制，为路由过程提供支撑。

Result: 在逻辑推理基准上的实验表明，该框架在准确率和可验证推理路径方面均显著优于SOTA的提示方法和RAG基线。

Conclusion: 神经符号融合架构能够有效弥补纯LLM推理的不足，通过显式逻辑建模与动态符号求解器路由的结合，既可提升推理准确性，又能提供可验证的推理路径。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable proficiency in natural language understanding, yet they struggle with strict multi-step reasoning, frequently suffering from hallucinations and inconsistency. Existing solutions like Chain-of-Thought (CoT) lack rigorous verification mechanisms, while standard Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) often misses the complex, structural dependencies inherent in logical tasks. To bridge this gap, we propose a Neuro-Symbolic architecture that integrates a Logical Knowledge Graph (LKG) with dynamic solver routing. Specifically, we introduce an ontology-based LKG that treats logical rules and constraints as first-class topological nodes, enabling explicit modeling of dependencies extracted from text. We further design a Logic Router to dynamically dispatch tasks to the optimal symbolic engine, which is supported by a topology-aware hybrid retrieval mechanism. Experimental results on logical reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that our framework significantly outperforms state-of-the-art prompting and RAG baselines, delivering higher accuracy and verifiable reasoning paths.

</details>


### [155] [LiveSim: Simulating Environment-Shaped Users in Multi-Agent Live-Stream Ecosystems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26849)
*Jiaqi Xu,Yiran Qiao,Jing Chen,Qiwei Zhong,Xiang Ao,Xueqi Cheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LiveSim是一个基于大语言模型的直播生态系统模拟框架，将用户表示为可编辑的行为假设，并通过轨迹引导的交互不断细化这些假设，将模拟与真实轨迹之间的差异提取为可迁移的环境-行为模式并存入集体行为记忆，从而提升用户级行为保真度和生态系统级模拟能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于LLM的用户行为模拟器依赖从历史观测推断的静态用户画像，在直播这类社交密集型环境中表现不足，因为互动动态会不断重塑用户行为，静态画像无法捕捉这种动态环境影响。

Method: LiveSim将用户建模为可编辑的行为假设，通过轨迹引导的交互逐步细化假设；当模拟轨迹与真实轨迹出现偏差时，提取差异作为缺失的环境塑造信号，进一步将这些信号转化为可迁移的环境-行为模式，并累积到集体行为记忆中，同时支持用户级和生态系统级模拟。

Result: 在真实世界直播风控数据上验证了LiveSim的有效性，在提升用户级行为保真度和支持生态系统级风险演化与平台干预效果分析方面均取得了良好效果。

Conclusion: LiveSim通过将环境塑造信号显式建模并累积为可迁移的行为模式，解决了静态用户画像在直播等动态社交环境中的不足，为多智能体生态系统模拟提供了新的范式。

Abstract: User behavior simulation with large language models~(LLMs) is increasingly used to support multi-agent ecosystem simulation. Existing simulators typically rely on static user profiles inferred from historical observations, which become inadequate in socially intensive environments such as live streaming where interaction dynamics continuously reshape user behavior. We propose \textbf{LiveSim}, an LLM-based framework for live-stream ecosystem simulation. It represents users as editable behavioral hypotheses and progressively refines them through trajectory-grounded interactions, where discrepancies between simulated and observed trajectories reveal missing environmental shaping effects. These signals are further extracted as transferable environment-behavior patterns and accumulated in a collective behavioral memory to improve user-level behavioral fidelity and support ecosystem-level simulation. Experiments on real-world live-stream risk-control data validate the effectiveness of LiveSim in improving user-level behavioral fidelity and enabling ecosystem-level analysis of risk evolution and platform intervention effects.

</details>


### [156] [BekchiAI: Measuring, Observing, and Controlling LLM Agents in One Click](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26867)
*Mesut Toruk*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出BekchiAI，一个双面系统：一方面包含一套用于衡量大语言模型代理技能（而非单纯准确率）的基准测试（BekchiAI-Benchmark），另一方面提供用于观测和控制已部署代理的Web平台（BekchiAI-Platform）。基准包含13个工具型ReAct代理、7个任务类别、共2057个确定性任务，全部经过验证器检查，并定义了超越准确率的行为指标（工具调用遵从、URL幻觉、源匹配和代币成本），最终在四个模型上进行了比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型代理在多种场景下推理、调用工具并自主行动，但其代理技能（如正确排序工具、在依赖关系下规划、判断不可信输入、对生成的论点进行接地验证）难以用仅基于准确率的排行榜来衡量。需要一个能够评估代理技能深度的基准，以及一个能够观测和控制实际运行代理的平台。

Method: 方法包含两个部分：（1）BekchiAI-Benchmark：构建了13个使用ReAct范式的工具调用代理，覆盖7个任务类别（算术、结构化/SQL、安全检测、URL接地、规划、编排和工具策略），共2057个确定性测试任务。每项任务的黄金答案通过运行标准SQL查询真实数据库、计算有向无环图（DAG）的精确调度、或评估闭式lambda函数获得。特别加入了对抗性安全样本，配以故意不完美的签名扫描器，使评分反映模型自身判断而非复制预言机。定义了超越准确率的行为指标：工具调用遵从率、URL幻觉与源匹配率、每模型代币成本。（2）BekchiAI-Platform：一个基于Web的观测与控制层，提供完整的代币和延迟遥测以及远程运行终止功能。

Result: 在四个模型（Qwen3.7-Max、gemma-4-31B-it、gemma4:26b、gpt-oss-120b）上进行了对比实验。实验发现关键在于同族模型之间的差异而非聚合结果。基准运行通过提供的评估脚本执行。

Conclusion: BekchiAI同时解决了代理技能测量基准与代理观测/控制平台两个层面的问题。基准和评估工具、平台均已公开发布，供研究社区使用。

Abstract: Large language model agents reason, call tools, and act autonomously over many steps, but their agentic skills-correctly sequencing tools, planning under dependencies, judging untrusted inputs, and grounding generated arguments-are hard to measure with accuracy-only leaderboards. We present BekchiAI, which addresses both sides: a benchmark for measuring agentic skill and a platform for observing and controlling live agents. The BekchiAI-Benchmark, a suite of 13 tool-using ReAct agents across 7 task categories (arithmetic, structured/SQL, security detection, URL grounding, planning, orchestration, and tool-policy), totalling 2,057 deterministic, committed test tasks. Every task is verifier-checkable gold answers are computed by running canonical SQL against a real database, computing the exact schedule of a directed acyclic graph (DAG), or evaluating closed-form lambdas including adversarial security samples paired with deliberately imperfect signature scanners so a score reflects the model's own judgment, not the copying of an oracle. We define a small set of behavioral metrics beyond accuracy-tool-call adherence, URL hallucination and source-match, and per-model token cost and report a four-model comparison (Qwen3.7-Max, gemma-4-31B-it, gemma4:26b, gpt-oss-120b) whose story is in the per-family spread, not the aggregate. The benchmark runs are executed using the provided evaluation scripts. BekchiAI-Platform is a complementary web-based observability and control layer for deployed agents, providing full token and latency telemetry as well as remote run termination. The benchmark, evaluation tools, and platform are publicly released.

</details>


### [157] [C-Unseen: Weak Signal Detection in Dynamic Temporal Knowledge Graphs via LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26870)
*Yassir Lairgi,Ludovic Moncla,Khalid Benabdeslem,Rémy Cazabet,Pierre Cléau*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出C-Unseen框架，利用动态时序知识图谱（DTKG）检测弱信号，将其定义为在连续快照中增殖的罕见、语义连贯的子图。框架包含两个模块：LLM驱动的罕见子图提取器和弱信号警报器，分别通过思维链推理识别与主流叙事相悖的子图，并跨时间步追踪其持续性。实验表明优于关键词、主题和图基线的检测方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有弱信号检测方法依赖于关键词频率、主题建模或未类型化图拓扑，无法捕捉弱信号所体现的语义与关系结构。作者旨在提出一种能够捕获语义与关系结构的方法，以更好地在重大变化成为定局前识别早期低可见性信号。

Method: 提出C-Unseen框架，包含两个模块：(1) 罕见子图提取器（Rare Subgraphs Extractor），利用LLM通过思维链推理识别与快照主流叙事存在张力（tension）的子图；(2) 弱信号警报器（Weak Signal Alerter），跨时间步追踪这些罕见子图的持续性，以隔离真正的弱信号。弱信号被定义为在连续TKG快照中增殖的罕见、语义连贯的子图。

Result: 实验结果表明C-Unseen在弱信号检测任务上优于基于关键词、主题和图的基线方法。

Conclusion: C-Unseen通过将弱信号建模为DTKG中跨快照持续增殖的罕见语义子图，并利用LLM的推理能力与时间维度上的持续性追踪，实现了自可解释（self-interpretable）的弱信号检测，验证了结合语义推理与时序关系的有效性。

Abstract: Weak signals are early, low-visibility indicators that precede significant changes before those changes become established. Existing detection methods, based on keyword frequency, topic modeling, or untyped graph topology, fail to capture the semantic and relational structure through which such signals manifest. In this paper, we propose C-Unseen, a self-interpretable framework for weak signal detection in Dynamic Temporal Knowledge Graphs (DTKGs). We define a weak signal as a rare, semantically coherent subgraph that proliferates across consecutive TKG snapshots. The framework operates through two modules: a Rare Subgraphs Extractor, in which an LLM identifies subgraphs whose content is in tension with the dominant snapshot narrative via chain-of-thought reasoning, and a Weak Signal Alerter, in which the persistence of these rare subgraphs is tracked across time steps to isolate true weak signals. Experimental results demonstrate that C-Unseen outperforms keyword-, topic-, and graph-based baselines.

</details>


### [158] [Evaluating human and LLM screening workflows in a conceptually complex scoping review: Recall--workload trade-offs and run-to-run consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26885)
*Nikol Figalová,Lynn Huestegge,Anne Böckler-Raettig*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在一项预注册的概念复杂范围综述中，比较了人类和大语言模型（LLM）的标题与摘要筛选工作流。结果显示，没有任何工作流能找回所有已验证的合格记录；人类工作流和GPT-5.4文件批处理保留42.2-45.0%的记录并达到82.3-82.9%的召回率，Gemini 3.1文件批处理召回率最高（83.9%）但保留56.7%记录。两个名义上相同的GPT-5.4运行在91.7%记录上一致，但差异了94条记录（含29条仅单一运行保留的合格记录）。结论是LLM筛选性能取决于实现的工作流而不仅是模型本身，对高召回任务LLM更适合用于经过验证、可审计、人类监督的工作流而非自主排除。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型越来越多地被用于证据综合中的筛选环节，而假阴性会使得相关研究在全文评估之前就被移除。现有研究缺乏在概念复杂综述中对人类与LLM筛选工作流进行系统比较的实证证据，尤其是可复现性、运行间一致性以及工作流配置对性能的影响。

Method: 在一项预注册研究中，对1,131条记录在保守标题筛选后进行对比：一位综述负责人、四位训练有素的助手（非重叠子集）以及七次完整的LLM运行（使用不同模型和不同处理配置，包括一次名义上相同的重复运行）。比较了保留工作量、针对316条已验证合格记录的运行召回率、一致性、运行间一致性和程序负担。由于资格仅在父综述中推进和评估的记录中得以验证，召回率为运行性估计。

Result: 没有任何工作流恢复所有已验证合格记录。人类工作流和两次GPT-5.4文件批处理保留了42.2-45.0%的记录并实现82.3-82.9%的召回率。Gemini 3.1文件批处理达到最高召回率（83.9%）但保留了56.7%的记录。全量一次处理的配置比对应文件批处理配置找回的合格记录更少。两次名义上相同的GPT-5.4文件批处理在91.7%的记录上一致，但在94条记录上存在差异，其中29条是仅被其中一次运行保留的已验证合格记录。

Conclusion: LLM筛选性能取决于实现的工作流而非仅模型身份本身。处理配置、工作量、记录级变化以及人机决策整合是已部署系统的实质性属性。对于高召回任务，LLM更适合用于经过验证、可审计、人类监督的工作流，而非自主排除。

Abstract: Background. Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for screening in evidence synthesis, where false negatives can remove relevant studies before full-text assessment. We compared human and LLM title-and-abstract screening workflows in a preregistered study embedded in a conceptually complex scoping review.
  Methods. After a conservative title-only screen, 1,131 records were screened by one review lead, four trained assistants screening non-overlapping subsets, and seven complete LLM runs using different models and processing configurations, including a nominally identical repeat run. We compared retained workload, operational recall against 316 verified eligible records, agreement, run-to-run consistency, and procedural burden. Because eligibility was verified only for records advanced and assessed in the parent review, recall estimates were operational.
  Results. No workflow recovered all verified eligible records. The human workflows and two GPT-5.4 file-batch runs retained 42.2-45.0% of records while achieving 82.3-82.9% recall. Gemini 3.1 file batches achieved the highest recall (83.9%) but retained 56.7% of records. All-at-once configurations recovered fewer eligible records than corresponding file-batch configurations. Two nominally identical GPT-5.4 file-batch runs agreed on 91.7% of records but differed on 94 records, including 29 verified eligible records retained by only one run.
  Discussion. LLM screening performance depended on the implemented workflow, not model identity alone. Processing configuration, workload, record-level variation, and human-LLM decision integration are therefore substantive properties of deployed systems. For high-recall tasks, LLMs are better suited to validated, auditable, human-supervised workflows than autonomous exclusion.

</details>


### [159] [Learning-Augmented Online Allocation under Unreliable Advice: Robustness, Exposure Fairness, and Distribution Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26889)
*Fredy Pokou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究带预测（learning-augmented）的在线分配问题，提出一种结合建议、保守回退和公平修正的鲁棒公平规则，在有限备选集、不可逆决策和曝光约束下，证明了在有界误差假设下的一致性和鲁棒性，且损失与预测误差成正比，实验验证了在对抗性建议下的稳定性和曝光差异的显著降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线分配问题中，学习增强算法虽能利用预测改善决策，但不可靠的建议可能损害效率和公平性。需要设计一种规则，既能利用预测优势，又能在建议出错时保持鲁棒性，同时兼顾公平。

Method: 提出一个结合"建议+保守回退+公平修正"的三元规则：将预测建议作为主要信号，配置保守的兜底策略以应对建议失效，并引入公平性修正机制来平衡曝光约束下的分配公平性。理论分析基于有界误差假设。

Result: 理论上证明了该规则的一致性（建议准确时性能良好）和鲁棒性（建议错误时损失与预测误差成正比）；实验表明在对抗性建议场景下保持稳定性，并显著降低了曝光差异（exposure disparity）。

Conclusion: 该规则在在线分配中实现了效率、鲁棒性与公平性的平衡，为带预测的在线决策提供了一种可靠且公平的实用方案。

Abstract: Learning-augmented algorithms improve online decisions using predictions, but unreliable advice may harm efficiency and fairness. We study an online allocation problem with finite candidate sets, irreversible decisions, and exposure constraints. We propose a robust and fair rule combining advice with a conservative fallback and fairness correction. Under bounded-error assumptions, we prove consistency and robustness with loss proportional to prediction error. Experiments show stability under adversarial advice and significant reductions in exposure disparity.

</details>


### [160] [AI agents in Algorithmic Electricity Markets: On the Emergence of Tacit Collusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26896)
*Jakub Seredyński,Georgios Tsaousoglou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了电力市场中基于多智能体强化学习的自主竞价代理是否可能通过独立学习产生默契合谋（tacit collusion）的问题。作者将策略性竞价建模为具有不完全公开监测的重复博弈，提出了一套超越利润对比的多维判定标准，并通过多智能体强化学习实验证明：即使代理从未被指示合谋，它们仍可能学习到维持超竞争水平结果的策略，显示出默契合谋的危险信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着学习型代理在电力市场竞价策略中的广泛采用，电力市场正变得日益算法化。其他算法化市场的证据表明，纯通过独立学习即可产生默契合谋；而电力市场本身具有寡头垄断和少量参与者重复互动的结构特征，使其在结构上容易受到非竞争行为的影响。因此需要检验学习算法是否可能在电力市场中诱发默契合谋的风险。

Method: 将策略性竞价建模为具有不完全公开监测的重复博弈；采用多智能体强化学习（MARL）模拟参与者的涌现行为；提出一套多维度判定标准（超越与纳什均衡的利润比较）来评估所得行为是否构成默契合谋。

Result: 实验结果表明这种危险在电力市场中是现实存在的：存在若干案例中，代理确实学会了维持超竞争（supra-competitive）结果，且这些结果符合默契合谋指标，尽管代理从未被指示进行合谋。

Conclusion: 学习型竞价代理在电力市场中可能在无明确指令下自发涌现默契合谋行为，这对电力市场的竞争性和监管设计提出了新的挑战，需要引起政策制定者和市场监管方的重视。

Abstract: As electricity market participants increasingly adopt learning-based agents for their bidding strategies, electricity markets are becoming algorithmic. Evidence from algorithmic markets in other domains shows that tacit collusion can arise purely through independent learning. Moreover, electricity markets are typically oligopolistic and feature repeated interaction among a small number of participants, making them structurally susceptible to non-competitive behavior. In the face of these observations, this paper investigates the hypothesis that tacit collusion may emerge in electricity markets where participants' actions are controlled by autonomous learning-based algorithms. We model strategic bidding as a repeated game with imperfect public monitoring, and model the participants' emergent behavior using multi-agent reinforcement learning. We propose a multi-dimensional set of criteria (going beyond profit comparisons against Nash equilibria) to assess whether the resulting behavior constitutes tacit collusion. Our experimental results showcase that such a danger is realistic for electricity markets: there are cases where agents do learn to sustain supra-competitive outcomes that are supportive of tacit collusion indicators, even though the agents were never instructed to collude.

</details>


### [161] [Counterfactual Bias Testing for Application Tracking System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26899)
*Sai Yashwant,Shruti Bansal,Anurag Dubey,Samaroha Chatterjee,Satyam Kumar,Shreyash Gupta,Gantala Thulsiram*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于任务专用LLM代理的自动化候选人-职位匹配审计方法论，通过生成身份中立简历并注入五种受保护特征（性别、年龄、居住地、语言、残疾）的处理，构建K×(1+N)对应审计矩阵，结合九指标公平性套件（反事实、群体公平、基于绩效）进行多维度偏差检测，最终输出自动化的PASS/INVESTIGATE/FAIL报告。实验表明单一分数或top-K指标会遗漏排名稳定性和nDCG边界问题，论证了多指标、多家族审计的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 候选人-职位匹配系统在新兴法规下被列为高风险AI，但传统人工对应审计方法（手工制作简历和手动提交）成本高昂、无法扩展到快速的流水线重训练周期，亟需一种可扩展、可复用的自动化审计方法论。

Method: (1) 使用任务专用LLM代理合成身份中立的基础简历，并跨五个受保护特征轴注入受控人口统计处理，生成K×(1+N)对应审计矩阵；(2) 依据符合EU AI法案的提示词定性标记推断的受保护特征；(3) 通过微调句嵌入模型和余弦相似度对候选人按职位描述排名；(4) 计算涵盖反事实（分数差、平均绝对排名变化、翻转率）、群体公平（top-K保留、五分之四/影响比）和绩效感知（Recall@K、nDCG@K、平等机会、均等几率）三大类的九指标公平套件，配备自助置信区间、显著性检验和Benjamini-Hochberg校正，最终生成自动化报告和综合风险评分。

Result: 在5个职位订单、100个基础候选人、10个人口统计处理的示例语料库（90次指标×变体评估）上：分数偏移、top-K保留和绩效感知率差距均在容差范围内，但排名稳定性指标（MARC）和nDCG@K各自暴露出边界发现——包括中性基线本身的一个发现——这些是仅看分数或保留率无法捕获的。

Conclusion: 研究结果支持多指标、多家族审计优于任何单一聚合分数，并验证了LLM代理生成的审计作为人工策划审计的实际低成本补充方案，适用于任何候选人-职位匹配流水线。

Abstract: Automated candidate-job matching systems are increasingly classified as high-risk AI under emerging regulation, yet auditing them for demographic bias is expensive: classical correspondence-audit studies require hand-crafted resumes and manual submission, which does not scale to fast pipeline retraining cycles. This paper presents a general, reusable methodology that (1) uses task-specialized LLM agents to synthesize identity-neutral base resumes and inject controlled demographic treatments across five protected-characteristic axes (sex/gender, age, residence, language, disability), producing a K x (1+N) correspondence-audit matrix; (2) qualitatively flags inferred protected characteristics per an EU AI Act-aligned prompt; (3) ranks candidates against a job description via a fine-tuned sentence-embedding model and cosine similarity; and (4) computes a nine-metric fairness suite spanning counterfactual (score delta, mean absolute rank change, flip rate), group-fairness (top-K retention, four-fifths/impact ratio), and merit-aware (Recall@K, nDCG@K, equal opportunity, equalized odds) families, each with bootstrap confidence intervals, significance tests, and Benjamini-Hochberg correction, culminating in an automated PASS/INVESTIGATE/FAIL report with a composite risk score. On an example corpus of 5 job orders, 100 base candidates, and 10 demographic treatments (90 metric x variant evaluations): score shifts, top-K retention, and merit-aware rate gaps stay within tolerance for every treatment, but a rank-stability metric (MARC) and nDCG@K each surface borderline findings - including one on the neutral baseline itself - that a score- or retention-only view would miss. The results argue for multi-metric, multi-family auditing over any single aggregate score, and for LLM-agent-generated audits as a practical, low-cost complement to human-curated audits for any candidate-job matching pipeline.

</details>


### [162] [A Table Is Worth 64 Tokens: Pixel-level Compression for Multi-Table Document Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26949)
*Iñigo Alonso,Mirella Lapata*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究面向含多表格文档的视觉（光学）上下文压缩方法在问答任务中的表现。研究发现，表格以原生分辨率保存为图像时与文本性能效率相当，但降采样会损失可读性并导致模型用更长、更低效的推理链来补偿，抵消预期成本节省。然而高度降采样的表格仍足以判断其与问题是否相关。据此提出一种免训练两步法：先从像素压缩上下文中识别所需表格，再对原生分辨率表格进行推理，在长文档上节省41%总token且准确率提升7点，比最高效的单步压缩配置少用15%token且无精度损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实文档问答需要处理文本与表格交织的长输入。光学上下文压缩（将上下文表示为图像）有望降低token成本，但其对表格理解的影响仍不明确。作者希望系统研究像素级表格压缩在多表格文档问答中的效果。

Method: 评估五种VLM在两个基准和五种视觉token预算下像素级表格压缩的性能。发现高度降采样表格仍保留足够信号以识别相关性，据此提出免训练两步法：先通过像素压缩上下文识别回答问题所需的表格，再对原生分辨率表格进行推理。

Result: 原生分辨率图像表格与文本在性能和效率上相当；降采样会以更长、更低效的推理链为代价补偿可读性损失。所提两步法在长文档上节省41%总token并提升7个准确率点，且比最高效单步压缩配置少用15%token而无精度损失。

Conclusion: 像素级表格压缩存在"相关性识别"与"内容精读"之间的能力不对称性。利用这一特性，通过"先筛选后精读"的两步策略，可以在无需训练的情况下同时实现token节省和准确率提升，为长文档多表格问答中的视觉压缩提供了有效范式。

Abstract: Answering questions over real-world documents requires processing long inputs that interleave text with tables. Optical context compression, which represents context as images, promises to reduce token cost, but its effect on table understanding remains unclear. We study pixel-level table compression for question answering over documents with multiple tables, evaluating five VLMs across two benchmarks and five visual-token budgets. Representing tables as images at native resolution matches text in both performance and efficiency, but downscaling them makes models compensate the loss in readability with longer, less effective reasoning traces that cancel the expected savings. Highly downscaled tables, however, preserve enough signal to identify whether they are relevant to a question. We exploit this asymmetry with a training-free, two-step method: the model first identifies the tables needed to answer a question from a pixel-compressed context, and then reasons over those at native resolution. On long documents, our method saves 41% of total tokens and gains 7 accuracy points over single-step QA with native resolution tables. It also uses 15% fewer tokens than the most efficient single-step compressed configuration, with no accuracy loss.

</details>


### [163] [From Atomic to Agentic: Towards Interpretable Evaluation of LLMs' Agentic Mathematical Capabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26950)
*Jiayi Kuang,Yinghui Li,Yunze Song,Keyu Chen,Zhifeng Shen,Yangning Li,Yidong Wang,Di Yin,Ruizhi Qiao,Xing Sun,Kai Jin,Ying Shen,Liang Lin,Philip S. Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个过程级（process-level）数学基准，用于评估LLM在数学推理中的智能体（agentic）能力，而非仅评估最终答案。该框架将问题求解行为与可复用的数学原子能力分类体系对齐，设计覆盖文本与多模态的规划、行动、反馈任务，并通过自动化流程生成高质量轨迹和细粒度标注。实验表明，最终答案准确率相似的模型可能具有截然不同的智能体能力画像，凸显过程级评估的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数学基准大多只评估最终答案（结果导向），缺乏对过程级错误或逻辑严谨性的诊断价值，难以指导LLM从端到端数学推理向稳健数学智能体转变。作者认为需要一种过程级评估框架来揭示模型在推理过程中的深层能力缺陷。

Method: 构建过程级基准框架：(1) 将智能体问题求解行为与可复用的数学原子能力分类体系（taxonomy）对齐；(2) 设计覆盖文本与多模态两种情境下的规划（planning）、行动（action）、反馈（feedback）三类任务；(3) 开发自动化数据生成管线，通过受控的LLM重写合成高质量轨迹，并生成细粒度标注。

Result: 实验发现，端到端准确率相近的模型可能展现出显著不同的智能体能力画像（agentic capability profiles），说明仅靠最终结果无法反映模型真实的过程级数学推理能力。

Conclusion: 过程级评估对于解释LLM的真实潜能至关重要，能有效指导下一代数学智能体的开发与优化。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are evolving from performing end-to-end mathematical reasoning to integrating agentic intelligence. However, most existing math benchmarks evaluate only final answers. This outcome-oriented evaluation provides limited diagnostic value for identifying process-level failures or rigorous logic, failing to guide the transformation of LLMs into robust agents. To bridge this gap, we present a process-level benchmark designed to evaluate the inherent agentic mathematical reasoning abilities of LLMs. Our framework aligns problem-solving agentic behaviors with a structured taxonomy of reusable mathematical atomic capabilities. We design a comprehensive suite of planning, action, and feedback tasks across both textual and multimodal contexts, supported by an automated pipeline that synthesizes high-quality trajectories and produces fine-grained annotations via controlled LLM rewriting. Experiments reveal that models with similar end-to-end accuracy can exhibit markedly different agentic capability profiles. This demonstrates that process-level evaluation is crucial for interpreting the true potential of LLMs and guiding the development of next-generation mathematical agents.

</details>


### [164] [GraphMemix: Query-Aware Evidence Forests for Long-Term Multimodal Agent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26983)
*Geng Li,Yuhao Wang,Dong Li,Jianye Hao,Yuxin Peng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出GraphMemix，一个基于组合优化的图记忆框架，将多模态智能体的长期记忆组织建模为查询感知的证据森林构建问题，在精度与生命周期成本之间建立新的帕累托前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长期记忆方法要么依赖昂贵的与查询无关的离线摘要，要么使用朴素的嵌入相似度匹配导致上下文不完整和冗余，难以有效组织多模态智能体的长期记忆。

Method: 包含三个关键组件：(1)候选图构建——通过模式与语义关系扩展多视角种子记忆以获取查询感知的原始上下文；(2)证据效用与激活成本——解耦直接记忆支持与锚定条件关系验证以抑制冗余或冲突信息；(3)森林优化——在最大证据预算下联合选择森林格式的记忆上下文及其可靠的关系结构。

Result: 在四个长期多模态记忆基准上，使用不同的基础模型均取得显著改进，并建立了准确率与生命周期成本之间的新帕累托前沿。

Conclusion: 通过将长期记忆组织为查询相关的子图结构，GraphMemix避免了实质性的生命周期开销，并能恢复低相似度的补充性证据，为多模态智能体长期记忆管理提供了更高效且更准确的解决方案。
